 )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        \\\"--save_generations\\\", action=\\\"store_true\\\",\\n        help=\\\"Save generated texts for accuracy evaluation\\\",\\n    )\\n For each sparsity level (10%, 20%):\\n#      - Build importance-based masks (prune least important heads/KV positions)\\n#      - Compute per-phase perplexity under 4 conditions (dense/attn/kv/both)\\n#      - Compute per-phase Interaction Index\\n#   5. Bootstrap 95% CI per (phase, level)\\n#   6. Pass/fail judgment (CI overlap + sign reversal, OR logic)\\n#   7. Output structured JSON\\n#\\n# Usage:\\n#   python -m pacs.profiling.run_profiling \\\\\\n#     --model_path &lt;HF model ID or path> \\\\\\n#     --data_path &lt;MATH-500 JSON> \\\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef load_model_and_tokenizer(model_path: str, device: str = \\\"cuda\\\"):\\n    from transformers import tokenizer.pad_token is None:\\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\\n\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loading model from %s\\\", model_path)\\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\\n        model_path,\\n        torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\\n        device_map=device,\\n        trust_remote_code=True,\\n        attn_implementation=\\\"eager\\\",\\n    )\\n    model.eval()\\n    return model, tokenizer\\n\\n\\ndef load_data(data_path: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loading data from %s\\\", data_path)\\n    with open(data_path) as f:\\n        data = compute_conditioned_perplexities(\\n    ii_calc: InteractionIndexCalculator,\\n    input_ids: torch.Tensor,\\n    phase_token_indices: List[int],\\n    attn_head_mask: torch.Tensor,\\n    kv_mask: torch.Tensor,\\n    dense_ce_losses: torch.Tensor,\\n    seq_len: int,\\n) -> Dict[str, float]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Run 3 sparse forward passes; dense ppl from cached CE losses.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n phase_token_indices=phase_token_indices,\\n    )\\n    ppl_kv = ii_calc.forward_with_sparse_config(\\n        input_ids, kv_mask=kv_mask,\\n        phase_token_indices=phase_token_indices,\\n    )\\n Now I can see the critical code. The key is line ~419-427:
```python
attn_head_mask = InteractionIndexCalculator._select_heads_to_mask(
    num_layers, num_heads, h_s,
    importance=head_importance,
)
kv_mask = InteractionIndexCalculator._select_kv_to_evict(
    full_seq_len, k_s,
    importance=kv_importance,
)
```

When `use_importance = True`, `head_importance` and `kv_importance` are computed from the dense forward. 

When `use_importance = False`, both are `None` and the selection methods fall back to random selection. I need to check how `_select_kv_to_evict` handles the `None` case to understand where I'd inject recency-based selection instead.

ppl_dense=ppl_dense,\\n            ppl_attn_sparse=ppl_attn,\\n            ppl_kv_sparse=ppl_kv,\\n            ppl_both_sparse=ppl_both,\\n            r_attn=r_attn,\\n            r_kv=r_kv,\\n            r_both=r_both,\\n            is_super_additive=ii > 0,\\n            sparsity_level=sparsity_level,\\n            num_tokens=num_tokens,\\n        )\\n\\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\\n    # Per-phase Interaction Index\\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\n    def compute_per_phase_ii(\\n        self,\\n        input_ids: torch.Tensor,\\n        phases: List,\\n        sparsity_level: float = 0.3,\\n    ) -> Dict[str, InteractionResult]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute II separately for each phase (reasoning / execution / transition).\\n\\n        Args:\\n            phases: List of Phase objects with .name and .token_indices attributes.\\n            sparsity_level: Fraction of heads to mask / KV positions to evict.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            Dict mapping phase name to its InteractionResult.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        results: Dict[str, InteractionResult] = {}\\n\\n        for phase in phases:\\n            result = self.compute_interaction_index(\\n                input_ids,\\n                sparsity_level=sparsity_level,\\n                phase_token_indices=phase.token_indices,\\n            )\\n            result.phase_name = phase.name\\n            results[phase.name] = result\\n\\n        return results\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Bootstrap CI for Interaction Index\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef bootstrap_interaction_index(\\n    ii_samples: List[float],\\n    n_resamples: int = 1000,\\n    ci_level: float = 0.95,\\n    seed: int = 42,\\n) -> Dict:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Bootstrap confidence intervals for mean and median II.\\n\\n    Returns dict with mean/ci_low/ci_high (mean-based) and\\n    median/median_ci_low/median_ci_high (median-based, primary).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n    samples = np.array(ii_samples)\\n    n = len(samples)\\n    boot_means = np.empty(n_resamples)\\n    boot_medians = np.empty(n_resamples)\\n    for i in range(n_resamples):\\n        idx = rng.randint(0, n, size=n)\\n        boot_means[i] = samples[idx].mean()\\n        boot_medians[i] = np.median(samples[idx])\\n    alpha = 1.0 - ci_level\\n    lo_pct = 100 * alpha / 2\\n    hi_pct = 100 * (1 - alpha / 2)\\n    return {\\n        \\\"mean\\\": float(samples.mean()),\\n        \\\"ci_low\\\": float(np.percentile(boot_means, lo_pct)),\\n        \\\"ci_high\\\": float(np.percentile(boot_means, hi_pct)),\\n        \\\"median\\\": float(np.median(samples)),\\n        \\\"median_ci_low\\\": float(np.percentile(boot_medians, lo_pct)),\\n        \\\"median_ci_high\\\": float(np.percentile(boot_medians, hi_pct)),\\n    }\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.45}}"
